		}
		remove_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						var = FPR_black_army_experience_var
						value = 0.3
						compare = less_than
					}
				}
				FPR_raise_insurgent_army_green = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						var = FPR_black_army_experience_var
						value = 0.3
						compare = greater_than_or_equals
					}
					check_variable = {
						var = FPR_black_army_experience_var
						value = 0.5
						compare = less_than
					}
				}
				FPR_raise_insurgent_army_regular = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						var = FPR_black_army_experience_var
						value = 0.5
						compare = greater_than_or_equals
					}
					check_variable = {
						var = FPR_black_army_experience_var
						value = 0.7
						compare = less_than
					}
				}
				FPR_raise_insurgent_army_seasoned = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						var = FPR_black_army_experience_var
						value = 0.7
						compare = greater_than_or_equals
					}
					check_variable = {
						var = FPR_black_army_experience_var
						value = 0.9
						compare = less_than
					}
				}
				FPR_raise_insurgent_army_veteran = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						var = FPR_black_army_experience_var
						value = 0.9
						compare = greater_than_or_equals
					}
					check_variable = {
						var = FPR_black_army_experience_var
						value = 1
						compare = less_than
					}
				}

	FPR_cause_a_scene_decision = {
		icon = GFX_FPR_Stoke_Revolution_In_decision
		allowed = {
			tag = FPR
		}
		visible = {
			has_completed_focus = FPR_Export_Anarchism_throughout_Europe
		}
		available = {
			FROM = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = {
						party_pop_array^18 > 0.33
					}
					has_stability < 0.2
				}
				NOT = {
					has_country_leader_ideology = left_anarchist
					is_in_faction_with = SOV
				}
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 2
		}
		fire_only_once = yes
		on_map_mode = map_only
		targets = {
			AUS
			BEL
			BUL
			CRO
			CYP
			CZE
			DEN
			EST
			FIN
			GRE
			HUN
			IRE
			ITA
			LIT
			LAT
			LUX
			HOL
			PRP
			ROM
			SLO
			SLV
			RSR
			SWE
			ENG
		}
		custom_cost_trigger = {
			has_equipment = {
				infantry_equipment > 999
			}
		}
		custom_cost_text = decision_cost_ie_1000
		complete_effect = {
			hidden_effect = {
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = infantry_weapons_type
					amount = -1000
				}
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			puppet = FROM
			FROM = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_ai = yes
					}
					set_variable = {
						ruling_party = 18
					}
					set_variable = {
						DSW_FRA_tfr_party = 18
					}
					set_variable = {
						DSW_FRA_scripted_elections = 0
					}
					set_variable = {
						disable_elections = 1
					}
					clear_array = gov_coalition_array
					set_country_flag = DSW_FRA_tfr_active
					set_politics = {
						ruling_party = neutrality
						elections_allowed = no
						last_election = "2017.4.23"
						election_frequency = 72
					}
					hidden_effect = {
						DSW_FRA_refresh_party_leader = yes
					}
					create_country_leader = {
						picture = "gfx/leaders/GMA/antifa_generic_1.png"
						ideology = neutral_Social
					}
					random_list = {
						seed = random
						959 = {
							set_cosmetic_tag = FPR_anarchist_collective_1
						}
						959 = {
							set_cosmetic_tag = FPR_anarchist_collective_2
						}
						959 = {
							set_cosmetic_tag = FPR_anarchist_collective_3
						}
					}

	FAF_demand_spanish_unification_decision = {
		visible = {
			tag = FAF
			has_completed_focus = FAF_Proclaim_the_Empire
			NOT = {
				970 = {
					is_owned_by = FAF
					is_core_of = FAF
				}
			}
		}
		icon = GFX_decision_FAF_Demand_Spanish_Unification
		days_remove = 90
		fire_only_once = yes
		cost = 100
		complete_effect = {

		}
		remove_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					SNS = {
						is_in_faction_with = FAF
					}
				}
				SNS = {
					country_event = {
						id = latinist.10
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						SPR = {
							is_in_faction_with = FAF
						}
						SNS = {
							is_in_faction_with = FAF
						}
					}
				}
				SPR = {
					country_event = {
						id = latinist.10
					}
				}
				SNS = {
					country_event = {
						id = latinist.10
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				declare_war_on = {
					target = RSR
					type = annex_everything
				}
				declare_war_on = {
					target = SPR
					type = annex_everything
				}
				declare_war_on = {
					target = SNS
					type = annex_everything
				}
			}
		}
	}
